### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  if [ "$slug" = "security" ]; then
    required_sec=(00-deepsearch-blueprint 01-brd-security 02-srs-secure-spec 03-sdd-hardening-architecture 04-fmea-security)
    missing=()
    for doc in "${required_sec[@]}"; do
      [ -f "docs/bok/security/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
    done
    [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ security — BoK complete (ciso-4-artifact)" || echo "⚠️ security — missing: ${missing[*]}"
    continue
  fi
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

> 3443	
> 3444	**v6.20.4 (2026-06-02)** — **Seal de conhecimento — zero código de produção tocado** (working tree limpo do início ao fim). > 3446	**#1 — disconnect verificado NON-DESTRUTIVO na UI logada.** Loguei como Usuário Zero via Supabase **Admin API `generate_link`** (magic link — método per-user reproduzível, sem token do DevTools nem senha crua) dirigindo `agent-browser` 0.27.0 (Chromium real). `/dashboard/social` renderiza as 3 contas ativas (LinkedIn/Instagram/Facebook), cada uma com botão Desconectar **interativo** (snapshot refs e11/e12/e13) wired a `rpc('disconnect_social')`; `useMetaConfig.ts:78`/`useSocialAccounts.ts:43` chamam `disconnect_meta`/`disconnect_social` (zero `.delete()` resquício). **Nenhum clique destrutivo** — as 3 contas seguem `is_active`; o clique→RPC→revogação-Vault já era DB-proven na v6.20.3. Fecha o resíduo "browser autenticado indisponível" da seal anterior.

> 3486	## Meta/Social Disconnect — Orphaned-Secret Revocation Record (2026-06-02)
> 3487	
> 3488	**v6.20.3 (2026-06-02)** — Sessão disparada por duas diretivas do Sovereign: (A) rodar `/security-review` **redundante** apontado para o `87c926f` (o guard de tenant meta/social selado em v6.20.2) — cinto-e-suspensório; e (B) atacar o débito de **segredo Vault órfão** que a própria seal v6.20.2 anotou no Pending. Padrão dominante mantido: **probe-first material** (Management API, todo teste mutante em `BEGIN…ROLLBACK` → zero mutação de produção) + **adversarial verify**. Antes de codar a Parte B, confirmei materialmente que o gap de tenant guard da diretiva original já estava 100% fechado em prod (`pg_get_functiondef` das 2 triggers + ataque comportamental 5/5 → `42501`), evitando uma migration redundante.
> 3489	
> 3490	**O que foi entregue (Parte B):**
> 3491	- **`disconnect_meta()` / `disconnect_social(p_account_id uuid)`** (migration `20260602160000`) — RPCs SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path=''`, fail-closed `28000` sem `auth.uid()`, EXECUTE só `authenticated`. Espelham `disconnect_wordpress`: deletam a linha **E** revogam o segredo Vault. Hooks `useMetaConfig`/`useSocialAccounts` migrados de `.delete()` → `.rpc()`; `types.ts` regenerado (3 entradas: as 2 RPCs + `vault_upsert_secret`).
> 3492	- **Guard de tenant por name-binding** (diferença do WP): segredos meta/social são nomeados pelo `id` da linha, não `user_id`. Cada `DELETE` no Vault é double-bound (`id = ref-da-própria-linha` **E** `name = '<prefix>_' || id-da-própria-linha`); como `id` é PK, o id do atacante nunca iguala o da vítima → impossível revogar segredo alheio. Defesa independente: o UUID bruto nunca vaza ao cliente.
> 3493	- **Limpeza one-time** dos órfãos preexistentes (`12 → 0` segredos Meta), provadamente orphan-scoped (`NOT EXISTS` linha base via `right(name,36)`), preservando os 3 segredos sociais vivos.

# ShakeHands — Session Pick-Up Protocol v3

Execute the ShakeHands /handson ritual for this project.

> **v3 (2026-05-08):** Added BoK Gate enforcement per MCORCH Master Execution Protocol — alert when active module work has no sealed BoK suite at `docs/bok/<slug>/`.

## PRE-FLIGHT (execute ALL in parallel before reading anything)

```bash
git log --oneline -7                        # recent history + commit style
git status --short                          # uncommitted changes
git diff HEAD --stat                        # change scope
npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | tail -20           # TypeScript strict check
docker ps --filter "name=mcorch" --format "{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}"
docker ps --filter "name=mega-brain" --format "{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}"
curl -s http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat  # Chroma API v2 health
ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null                    # BoK suites disponíveis
wc -l HANDOFF.md                            # total lines — drives the read-from-end offset
```

Read in parallel (HANDOFF.md uses **read-from-end strategy** — SSP-01 v6.5.0; arquivo monolítico newest-first em ~3170+ linhas, leitura completa estoura limite de 25k tokens):
- `HANDOFF.md` with `limit=71` → Task State header + FIRST ACTION FOR NEXT AGENT (sempre estável, contém summary da fase atual selada)
- `HANDOFF.md` with `offset=<total_lines - 400>` `limit=400` → últimas 1-2 Records + Pending Actions + GraphRAG State + Infrastructure + Key Files (suffix sections always at the tail)
- `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, data flow, key files — incluindo MCORCH Master Execution Protocol + Survival Laws link)
- `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (sprint goal, 4Cs snapshot, top gaps)
- `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/MEMORY.md` (memory index)

> ⚠️ **Auditoria histórica de seals antigos:** usar `Read` com offset arbitrário em HANDOFF.md (fora do fluxo padrão de pickup). Ordering newest-first: seal mais antigo ≈ linha 2805, seal mais recente logo após linha 71. Cada `## <Phase> Record (YYYY-MM-DD)` marca um seal. Manter o Read em chunks ≤ 400 linhas para preservar budget de tokens.

Also check for loose files in scratch/:
```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null && echo "⚠️ scratch/ has files — consider moving to .claude/scripts/db/" || echo "scratch/ clean ✅"
```

### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  if [ "$slug" = "security" ]; then
    required_sec=(00-deepsearch-blueprint 01-brd-security 02-srs-secure-spec 03-sdd-hardening-architecture 04-fmea-security)
    missing=()
    for doc in "${required_sec[@]}"; do
      [ -f "docs/bok/security/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
    done
    [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ security — BoK complete (ciso-4-artifact)" || echo "⚠️ security — missing: ${missing[*]}"
    continue
  fi
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> 84	## 🧱 Princípios de Harness (mandatórios em toda a suíte)
> 85	
> 86	1. **Separation of concerns:** hooks governam **estado e processamento de dados** (`useSpacesCanvas`, `useParamRegistry`, `useGenerationLedger`); componentes só fazem **orquestração visual**. Zero `fetch`/RPC dentro de `.tsx` de nó.
> 87	2. **Type-safe ports:** conexões só entre portas compatíveis (`image|text|video|audio|svg`). Validação em `zod` antes do edge entrar no store.
> 88	3. **Ledger-first:** nenhum nó executa sem passar pelo gate de `mcoCoins` (estimar → debitar → executar → reconciliar/refund).
> 89	4. **No context rot:** cada artefato referencia, nunca duplica. Definições canônicas de tipos vivem no `05-sdd`.
